On success it prints `deleted` and the id. An\nabsent id exits 4. A delete that leaves any account below zero exits 3. Every rejected delete\nchanges nothing.\n\n## Trigger\n\nThe user runs `ledger txn delete <id>`.\n\n## Behavior\n\n1. Parse `<id>` as an integer. A non-integer id exits 3 before any lookup.\n2. Within the command transaction, fetch the row by `txn_id` through `transactions.get`. An absent\n   id exits 4.\n3. Remove the row through `transactions.delete`.\n4. Apply the balance rule to the account the removed row touched (FEATURE-Balance-Rule.md). A\n   negative balance exits 3 and rolls back the delete.\n5. Commit and print:\n\n```text\ndeleted\n<id>\n```\n\n## Routes and Interfaces\n\n| Interface | Kind | Notes |\n|---|---|---|\n| `ledger txn delete <id>` | CLI command | Exit 0 on success, 3 on rejection, 4 when absent. |\n\n## Reads and Writes\n\n| Operation | Access | Repository operation | Notes |\n|---|---|---|---|\n| Fetch transaction | read | `transactions.get` | Absent id maps to exit 4. |\n| Delete transaction | write | `transactions.delete` | Runs inside the command transaction. |\n\n## Programmatic Acceptance\n\n=== AC txn-delete-removes-row ===\nIntent: Deleting an existing transaction removes it so a subsequent get reports absence.\nRequires: executable=python3; scope=test\n\nfrom persistence.testing import db_session\nfrom cli.testing import cli_runner\n\nwith db_session() as db:\n    db.accounts.create(account=\"TDA1\", name=\"Delete Test A\")\n    db.categories.create(category=\"TDC1\", description=\"Delete Test C\")\n    row = db.transactions.create(txn_id=9001, date=\"2026-05-01\", account=\"TDA1\",\n                                 category=\"TDC1\", amount=500, memo=\"to delete\")\n    code, out, err = cli_runner(\"txn\", \"delete\", \"9001\")\n    assert code == 0\n    code2, out2, err2 = cli_runner(\"txn\", \"get\", \"9001\")\n    assert code2 == 4\n=== END AC txn-delete-removes-row ===\n\n=== AC txn-delete-output ===\nIntent: A successful delete prints exactly the header-free output `deleted` then the id.\nRequires: executable=python3; scope=test\n\nfrom persistence.testing import db_session\nfrom cli.testing import cli_runner\n\nwith db_session() as db:\n    db.accounts.create(account=\"TDA2\", name=\"Delete Test B\")\n    db.categories.create(category=\"TDC2\", description=\"Delete Test D\")\n    db.transactions.create(txn_id=9002, date=\"2026-05-01\", account=\"TDA2\",\n                           category=\"TDC2\", amount=700, memo=\"output check\")\n    code, out, err = cli_runner(\"txn\", \"delete\", \"9002\")\n    assert code == 0\n    assert out.splitlines()[0] == \"deleted\"\n    assert out.splitlines()[1] == \"9002\"\n=== END AC txn-delete-output ===\n\n=== AC txn-delete-absent-id ===\nIntent: Deleting an absent id exits 4 and changes nothing.\nRequires: executable=python3; It stores two rows\nin category `TRANSFER`: `-amount` on `<from>` and `+amount` on `<to>`. Both rows are stored or\nneither is.\n\n## Trigger\n\nThe user runs `ledger transfer <from> <to> <amount> <date>`.\n\n## Behavior\n\n1. Validate syntax before any lookup: `<amount>` parses as a positive amount, and `<date>` is a\n   real `YYYY-MM-DD` date. Either failure exits 3.\n2. Require `<from>` and `<to>` to differ. Equal accounts exit 3.\n3. Within the command transaction, confirm both accounts exist through `accounts.get`. A missing\n   account exits 3, not 4.\n4. Take the next two ids: `n` and `n+1`, where `n` is one more than the largest `txn_id` present\n   (1 in an empty table), using the shared next-id function from FEATURE-Transaction-Post.md.\n5. Create the row `n` on `<from>` with amount `-amount` and memo `transfer to <to>`, and the row\n   `n+1` on `<to>` with amount `+amount` and memo `transfer from <from>`. Both use category\n   `TRANSFER`.\n6. Apply the balance rule to both touched accounts (FEATURE-Balance-Rule.md). A negative balance\n   exits 3 and rolls back both rows.\n7. Commit and print the two rows in CSV with the header:\n\n```text\ntxn_id,date,account,category,amount,memo\n<n>,<date>,<from>,TRANSFER,-<amount>,transfer to <to>\n<n+1>,<date>,<to>,TRANSFER,<amount>,transfer from <from>\n```\n\nAmounts are written with exactly two decimal places (FEATURE-Amount-Format.md).\n\n## Routes and Interfaces\n\n| Interface | Kind | Notes |\n|---|---|---|\n| `ledger transfer <from> <to> <amount> <date>` | CLI command | Exit 0 on success, 3 on rejection. |\n\n## Reads and Writes\n\n| Operation | Access | Repository operation | Notes |\n|---|---|---|---|\n| Confirm accounts | read | `accounts.get` | Missing account exits 3. |\n| Read largest id | read | `transactions.list` | Next id is derived from the rows, never stored. |\n| Insert the two rows | write | `transactions.create` | Both inserts run inside one command transaction. |\n\n## Programmatic Acceptance\n\n=== AC transfer-stores-two-rows ===\nIntent: A valid transfer stores two TRANSFER rows with the next two ids and the stated memos.\nRequires: executable=python3; scope=test\n\nfrom persistence.testing import db_session\nfrom cli.testing import cli_runner\n\nwith db_session() as db:\n    db.accounts.create(account=\"XFA1\", name=\"Transfer From\")\n    db.accounts.create(account=\"XFB1\", name=\"Transfer To\")\n    db.categories.create(category=\"TRANSFER\", description=\"Internal transfer\")\n    db.transactions.create(txn_id=8001, date=\"2026-05-01\", account=\"XFA1\",\n                           category=\"TRANSFER\", amount=5000, memo=\"seed\")\n    code, out, err = cli_runner(\"transfer\", \"XFA1\", \"XFB1\", \"100.00\", \"2026-05-02\")\n    assert code == 0\n    assert db.transactions.get(txn_id=8002).account == \"XFA1\"\n    assert db.transactions.get(txn_id=8002).amount == -10000\n    assert db.transactions.get(txn_id=8002).memo == \"transfer to XFB1\"\n    assert db.transactions.get(txn_id=8003).account == \"XFB1\"\n    assert db.transactions.get(txn_id=8003).amount == 10000\n    assert db.transactions.get(txn_id=8003).memo == \"transfer from XFA1\"\n=== END AC transfer-stores-two-rows ===\n\n=== AC transfer-output-rows ===\nIntent: A valid transfer prints the header and the two stored rows in CSV.\nRequires: executable=python3; no partial transfer is ever visible.\n- Transfer ids come from the shared next-id function, never from a separately stored counter.\n- A rejected transfer prints nothing to standard output.\n=== END ARTIFACT ===\n=== BEGIN ARTIFACT FEATURE-Report-Balances.md ===\n# Feature: Report Balances\n\n| Field       | Value |\n|-------------|-------|\n| Version     | 20261020 V1 |\n| Description | Print one balance row per account, ordered by code, then a TOTAL row, all computed from the database. |\n| Depends On  | DATABASE.md, FEATURE-Amount-Format.md, FEATURE-Command-Transaction.md, FEATURE-CLI-Output.md, FEATURE-CLI-Dispatch.md |\n| Provides    | ledger report balances |\n| Consumes    | accounts.list, transactions.list |\n\n## Questions\n\n- None.\n\n## Purpose\n\n`ledger report balances` reports each account's transaction count and balance, followed by a\nTOTAL row across all accounts.\n\n## Trigger\n\nThe user runs `ledger report balances`.\n\n## Behavior\n\n1. Within the command transaction, read every account through `accounts.list`, ordered by\n   `account` ascending.\n2. For each account, compute its transaction count and balance by query. The balance is the sum\n   of its transaction amounts in integer cents, formatted per FEATURE-Amount-Format.md.\n3. Print the header and one row per account:\n\n```text\naccount,name,transactions,balance\n```\n\n4. Print the TOTAL row. Its count is the total number of transactions, and its balance is the sum\n   of every transaction amount:\n\n```text\nTOTAL,,<count>,<sum>\n```\n\n5. With no accounts, print only `TOTAL,,0,0.00` after the header.\n\nNo value is embedded as a constant. Every count and sum is derived from a query.\n\n## Routes and Interfaces\n\n| Interface | Kind | Notes |\n|---|---|---|\n| `ledger report balances` | CLI command | Exit 0 on success. |\n\n## Reads and Writes\n\n| Operation | Access | Repository operation | Notes |\n|---|---|---|---|\n| List accounts | read | `accounts.list` | Ordered by code. |\n| List transactions | read | `transactions.list` | Aggregated per account and in total. |\n\n## Programmatic Acceptance\n\n=== AC report-balances-header ===\nIntent: The balances report begins with the account header line.\nRequires: executable=python3; scope=test\n\nfrom persistence.testing import db_session\nfrom cli.testing import cli_runner\n\nwith db_session() as db:\n    db.accounts.create(account=\"RBA1\", name=\"Report Balance A\")\n    code, out, err = cli_runner(\"report\", \"balances\")\n    assert code == 0\n    assert out.splitlines()[0] == \"account,name,transactions,balance\"\n=== END AC report-balances-header ===\n\n=== AC report-balances-empty ===\nIntent: With no accounts the report prints only the TOTAL row of zeros.\nRequires: executable=python3; scope=test\n\nfrom persistence.testing import db_session\nfrom cli.testing import cli_runner\n\nwith db_session() as db:\n    code, out, err = cli_runner(\"report\", \"balances\")\n    assert code == 0\n    assert out.splitlines()[1:] == [\"TOTAL,,0,0.00\"]\n=== END AC report-balances-empty ===\n\n=== AC report-balances-row-values ===\nIntent: Each account row carries its name, transaction count, and balance computed from its transactions.\nRequires: executable=python3; scope=test\n\nfrom persistence.testing import db_session\nfrom cli.testing import cli_runner\n\nwith db_session() as db:\n    db.accounts.create(account=\"RBB1\", name=\"Report Balance B\")\n    db.categories.create(category=\"RBC1\", description=\"Report Category\")\n    db.transactions.create(txn_id=7001, date=\"2026-05-01\", account=\"RBB1\",\n                           category=\"RBC1\", amount=1250, memo=\"a\")\n    db.transactions.create(txn_id=7002, date=\"2026-05-02\", account=\"RBB1\",\n                           category=\"RBC1\", amount=-250, memo=\"b\")\n    code, out, err = cli_runner(\"report\", \"balances\")\n    assert code == 0\n    rows = out.splitlines()\n    assert rows[1] == \"RBB1,Report Balance B,2,10.00\"\n=== END AC report-balances-row-values ===\n\n=== AC report-balances-ordered-by-code ===\nIntent: Account rows are ordered by account code ascending.\nRequires: executable=python3; scope=test\n\nfrom persistence.testing import db_session\nfrom cli.testing import cli_runner\n\nwith db_session() as db:\n    db.accounts.create(account=\"RBZ1\", name=\"Report Zed\")\n    db.accounts.create(account=\"RBY1\", name=\"Report Yankee\")\n    code, out, err = cli_runner(\"report\", \"balances\")\n    assert code == 0\n    rows = out.splitlines()\n    assert rows[1].startswith(\"RBY1,\")\n    assert rows[2].startswith(\"RBZ1,\")\n=== END AC report-balances-ordered-by-code ===\n\n=== AC report-balances-total-row ===\nIntent: The TOTAL row sums every account's transaction count and balance.\nRequires: executable=python3; scope=test\n\nfrom persistence.testing import db_session\nfrom cli.testing import cli_runner\n\nwith db_session() as db:\n    db.accounts.create(account=\"RBT1\", name=\"Report Total One\")\n    db.accounts.create(account=\"RBT2\", name=\"Report Total Two\")\n    db.categories.create(category=\"RBTC\", description=\"Report Total Category\")\n    db.transactions.create(txn_id=7101, date=\"2026-05-01\", account=\"RBT1\",\n                           category=\"RBTC\", amount=300, memo=\"one\")\n    db.transactions.create(txn_id=7102, date=\"2026-05-01\", account=\"RBT2\",\n                           category=\"RBTC\", amount=-100, memo=\"two\")\n    code, out, err = cli_runner(\"report\", \"balances\")\n    assert code == 0\n    assert out.splitlines()[-1] == \"TOTAL,,2,2.00\"\n=== END AC report-balances-total-row ===\n\n## User Acceptance\n\n- None.\n\n## Guardrails\n\n- No count, sum, or total is hardcoded; every value comes from a query.\n- Amounts are formatted with two decimals, never floating point, and a zero total prints `0.00`,\n  never `-0.00`.\n=== END ARTIFACT ===\n=== BEGIN ARTIFACT FEATURE-Report-Categories.md ===\n# Feature: Report Categories\n\n| Field       | Value |\n|-------------|-------|\n| Version     | 20261020 V1 |\n| Description | Print one row per category, including categories with no transactions, ordered by code, then a TOTAL row, all computed from the database. |\n| Depends On  | DATABASE.md, FEATURE-Amount-Format.md, FEATURE-Command-Transaction.md, FEATURE-CLI-Output.md, FEATURE-CLI-Dispatch.md |\n| Provides    | ledger report categories |\n| Consumes    | categories.list, transactions.list |\n\n## Questions\n\n- None.\n\n## Purpose\n\n`ledger report categories` reports, for every category, its description, transaction count,\ninflow, outflow, and net, followed by a TOTAL row.\n\n## Trigger\n\nThe user runs `ledger report categories`.\n\n## Behavior\n\n1. Within the command transaction, read every category through `categories.list`, ordered by\n   `category` ascending.\n2. For each category, compute by query over `transactions.list`:\n   - `transactions`: the number of rows in that category.\n   - `inflow`: the sum of positive amounts, in integer cents.\n   - `outflow`: the sum of the absolute values of negative amounts, in integer cents, printed as a\n     positive number.\n   - `net`: `inflow - outflow`.\n   A category with no transactions reports `0`, `0.00`, `0.00`, `0.00`.\n3. Print the header and one row per category:\n\n```text\ncategory,description,transactions,inflow,outflow,net\n```\n\n4. Print the TOTAL row, whose count, inflow, outflow, and net are summed over every transaction:\n\n```text\nTOTAL,,<count>,<inflow>,<outflow>,<net>\n```\n\nAmounts are formatted per FEATURE-Amount-Format.md. No value is embedded as a constant.\n\n## Routes and Interfaces\n\n| Interface | Kind | Notes |\n|---|---|---|\n| `ledger report categories` | CLI command | Exit 0 on success. |\n\n## Reads and Writes\n\n| Operation | Access | Repository operation | Notes |\n|---|---|---|---|\n| List categories | read | `categories.list` | Ordered by code; Within the command transaction, read every transaction through `transactions.list`.\n2. Group rows by month, the `YYYY-MM` prefix of `date`. Only months that have transactions appear.\n3. For each month, compute by query:\n   - `transactions`: the number of rows in that month.\n   - `inflow`: the sum of positive amounts, in integer cents.\n   - `outflow`: the sum of the absolute values of negative amounts, in integer cents, printed as a\n     positive number.\n   - `net`: `inflow - outflow`.\n4. Print the header, then one row per month in ascending `YYYY-MM` order:\n\n```text\nmonth,transactions,inflow,outflow,net\n```\n\n5. Print the TOTAL row, whose count, inflow, outflow, and net are summed over every transaction:\n\n```text\nTOTAL,<count>,<inflow>,<outflow>,<net>\n```\n\n6. With no transactions, print only `TOTAL,0,0.00,0.00,0.00` after the header.\n\nAmounts are formatted per FEATURE-Amount-Format.md. No value is embedded as a constant.\n\n## Routes and Interfaces\n\n| Interface | Kind | Notes |\n|---|---|---|\n| `ledger report monthly` | CLI command | Exit 0 on success. |\n\n## Reads and Writes\n\n| Operation | Access | Repository operation | Notes |\n|---|---|---|---|\n| List transactions | read | `transactions.list` | Grouped by month and totalled in code from the query result. |\n\n## Programmatic Acceptance\n\n=== AC report-monthly-header ===\nIntent: The monthly report begins with the month header line.\nRequires: executable=python3; scope=test\n\nfrom persistence.testing import db_session\nfrom cli.testing import cli_runner\n\nwith db_session() as db:\n    code, out, err = cli_runner(\"report\", \"monthly\")\n    assert code == 0\n    assert out.splitlines()[0] == \"month,transactions,inflow,outflow,net\"\n=== END AC report-monthly-header ===\n\n=== AC report-monthly-empty ===\nIntent: With no transactions the report prints only the TOTAL row of zeros.\nRequires: executable=python3; BEGIN ARTIFACT FEATURE-Transaction-Delete.md ===\n# Feature: Transaction Delete\n\n| Field       | Value |\n|-------------|-------|\n| Version     | 20261020 V1 |\n| Description | Delete one transaction by id, applying the non-negative balance rule after the delete. |\n| Depends On  | DATABASE.md, FEATURE-Balance-Rule.md, FEATURE-Command-Transaction.md, FEATURE-CLI-Output.md, FEATURE-CLI-Dispatch.md |\n| Provides    | ledger txn delete <id> |\n| Consumes    | transactions.get, transactions.delete |\n\n## Questions\n\n- None.\n\n## Purpose\n\n`ledger txn delete <id>` removes one transaction. On success it prints `deleted` and the id. An\nabsent id exits 4. A delete that leaves any account below zero exits 3. Every rejected delete\nchanges nothing.\n\n## Trigger\n\nThe user runs `ledger txn delete <id>`.\n\n## Behavior\n\n1. Parse `<id>` as an integer. A non-integer id exits 3 before any lookup.\n2. Within the command transaction, fetch the row by `txn_id` through `transactions.get`. An absent\n   id exits 4.\n3. Remove the row through `transactions.delete`.\n4. Apply the balance rule to the account the removed row touched (FEATURE-Balance-Rule.md). A\n   negative balance exits 3 and rolls back the delete.\n5. Commit and print:\n\n```text\ndeleted\n<id>\n```\n\n## Routes and Interfaces\n\n| Interface | Kind | Notes |\n|---|---|---|\n| `ledger txn delete <id>` | CLI command | Exit 0 on success, 3 on rejection, 4 when absent. |\n\n## Reads and Writes\n\n| Operation | Access | Repository operation | Notes |\n|---|---|---|---|\n| Fetch transaction | read | `transactions.get` | Absent id maps to exit 4. |\n| Delete transaction | write | `transactions.delete` | Runs inside the command transaction. |\n\n## Programmatic Acceptance\n\n=== AC txn-delete-removes-row ===\nIntent: Deleting an existing transaction removes it so a subsequent get reports absence.\nRequires: executable=python3; scope=test\n\nfrom persistence.testing import db_session\nfrom cli.testing import cli_runner\n\nwith db_session() as db:\n    db.accounts.create(account=\"TDA1\", name=\"Delete Test A\")\n    db.categories.create(category=\"TDC1\", description=\"Delete Test C\")\n    row = db.transactions.create(txn_id=9001, date=\"2026-05-01\", account=\"TDA1\",\n                                 category=\"TDC1\", amount=500, memo=\"to delete\")\n    code, out, err = cli_runner(\"txn\", \"delete\", \"9001\")\n    assert code == 0\n    code2, out2, err2 = cli_runner(\"txn\", \"get\", \"9001\")\n    assert code2 == 4\n=== END AC txn-delete-removes-row ===\n\n=== AC txn-delete-output ===\nIntent: A successful delete prints exactly the header-free output `deleted` then the id.\nRequires: executable=python3; scope=test\n\nfrom persistence.testing import db_session\nfrom cli.testing import cli_runner\n\nwith db_session() as db:\n    db.accounts.create(account=\"TDA2\", name=\"Delete Test B\")\n    db.categories.create(category=\"TDC2\", description=\"Delete Test D\")\n    db.transactions.create(txn_id=9002, date=\"2026-05-01\", account=\"TDA2\",\n                           category=\"TDC2\", amount=700, memo=\"output check\")\n    code, out, err = cli_runner(\"txn\", \"delete\", \"9002\")\n    assert code == 0\n    assert out.splitlines()[0] == \"deleted\"\n    assert out.splitlines()[1] == \"9002\"\n=== END AC txn-delete-output ===\n\n=== AC txn-delete-absent-id ===\nIntent: Deleting an absent id exits 4 and changes nothing.\nRequires: executable=python3; scope=test\n\nfrom persistence.testing import db_session\nfrom cli.testing import cli_runner\n\nwith db_session() as db:\n    db.accounts.create(account=\"TDA3\", name=\"Delete Test E\")\n    db.categories.create(category=\"TDC3\", description=\"Delete Test F\")\n    db.transactions.create(txn_id=9003, date=\"2026-05-01\", account=\"TDA3\",\n                           category=\"TDC3\", amount=1000, memo=\"receipt\")\n    db.transactions.create(txn_id=9004, date=\"2026-05-02\", account=\"TDA3\",\n                           category=\"TDC3\", amount=-400, memo=\"payment\")\n    code, out, err = cli_runner(\"txn\", \"delete\", \"9003\")\n    assert code == 3\n    assert db.transactions.get(txn_id=9003).amount == 1000\n=== END AC txn-delete-balance-rejection ===\n\n## User Acceptance\n\n- None.\n\n## Guardrails\n\n- A rejected delete changes no row and prints nothing to standard output.\n- Balance is computed from the database after the delete, never from a cached value.\n- Absent-id and non-integer-id checks run before any write.\n=== END ARTIFACT ===\n=== BEGIN ARTIFACT FEATURE-Transfer.md ===\n# Feature: Transfer\n\n| Field       | Value |\n|-------------|-------|\n| Version     | 20261020 V1 |\n| Description | Store a transfer as two TRANSFER rows, or neither, under the non-negative balance rule. |\n| Depends On  | DATABASE.md, FEATURE-Transaction-Post.md, FEATURE-Balance-Rule.md, FEATURE-Amount-Format.md, FEATURE-Date-Validation.md, FEATURE-Code-Validation.md, FEATURE-CLI-Output.md, FEATURE-CLI-Dispatch.md |\n| Provides    | ledger transfer <from> <to> <amount> <date> |\n| Consumes    | accounts.get, transactions.create, transactions.list |\n\n## Questions\n\n- None.\n\n## Purpose\n\n`ledger transfer <from> <to> <amount> <date>` moves cash between two accounts. It stores two rows\nin category `TRANSFER`: `-amount` on `<from>` and `+amount` on `<to>`. Both rows are stored or\nneither is.\n\n## Trigger\n\nThe user runs `ledger transfer <from> <to> <amount> <date>`.\n\n## Behavior\n\n1. Validate syntax before any lookup: `<amount>` parses as a positive amount, and `<date>` is a\n   real `YYYY-MM-DD` date. Either failure exits 3.\n2. Require `<from>` and `<to>` to differ. Equal accounts exit 3.\n3. Within the command transaction, confirm both accounts exist through `accounts.get`. A missing\n   account exits 3, not 4.\n4. Take the next two ids: `n` and `n+1`, where `n` is one more than the largest `txn_id` present\n   (1 in an empty table), using the shared next-id function from FEATURE-Transaction-Post.md.\n5. Create the row `n` on `<from>` with amount `-amount` and memo `transfer to <to>`, and the row\n   `n+1` on `<to>` with amount `+amount` and memo `transfer from <from>`. Both use category\n   `TRANSFER`.\n6. Apply the balance rule to both touched accounts (FEATURE-Balance-Rule.md). A negative balance\n   exits 3 and rolls back both rows.\n7. Commit and print the two rows in CSV with the header:\n\n```text\ntxn_id,date,account,category,amount,memo\n<n>,<date>,<from>,TRANSFER,-<amount>,transfer to <to>\n<n+1>,<date>,<to>,TRANSFER,<amount>,transfer from <from>\n```\n\nAmounts are written with exactly two decimal places (FEATURE-Amount-Format.md).\n\n## Routes and Interfaces\n\n| Interface | Kind | Notes |\n|---|---|---|\n| `ledger transfer <from> <to> <amount> <date>` | CLI command | Exit 0 on success, 3 on rejection. |\n\n## Reads and Writes\n\n| Operation | Access | Repository operation | Notes |\n|---|---|---|---|\n| Confirm accounts | read | `accounts.get` | Missing account exits 3. |\n| Read largest id | read | `transactions.list` | Next id is derived from the rows, never stored. |\n| Insert the two rows | write | `transactions.create` | Both inserts run inside one command transaction. |\n\n## Programmatic Acceptance\n\n=== AC transfer-stores-two-rows ===\nIntent: A valid transfer stores two TRANSFER rows with the next two ids and the stated memos.\nRequires: executable=python3; scope=test\n\nfrom persistence.testing import db_session\nfrom cli.testing import cli_runner\n\nwith db_session() as db:\n    db.accounts.create(account=\"XFA1\", name=\"Transfer From\")\n    db.accounts.create(account=\"XFB1\", name=\"Transfer To\")\n    db.categories.create(category=\"TRANSFER\", description=\"Internal transfer\")\n    db.transactions.create(txn_id=8001, date=\"2026-05-01\", account=\"XFA1\",\n                           category=\"TRANSFER\", amount=5000, memo=\"seed\")\n    code, out, err = cli_runner(\"transfer\", \"XFA1\", \"XFB1\", \"100.00\", \"2026-05-02\")\n    assert code == 0\n    assert db.transactions.get(txn_id=8002).account == \"XFA1\"\n    assert db.transactions.get(txn_id=8002).amount == -10000\n    assert db.transactions.get(txn_id=8002).memo == \"transfer to XFB1\"\n    assert db.transactions.get(txn_id=8003).account == \"XFB1\"\n    assert db.transactions.get(txn_id=8003).amount == 10000\n    assert db.transactions.get(txn_id=8003).memo == \"transfer from XFA1\"\n=== END AC transfer-stores-two-rows ===\n\n=== AC transfer-output-rows ===\nIntent: A valid transfer prints the header and the two stored rows in CSV.\nRequires: executable=python3; Within the command transaction, read every account through `accounts.list`, ordered by\n   `account` ascending.\n2. For each account, compute its transaction count and balance by query. The balance is the sum\n   of its transaction amounts in integer cents, formatted per FEATURE-Amount-Format.md.\n3. Print the header and one row per account:\n\n```text\naccount,name,transactions,balance\n```\n\n4. Print the TOTAL row. Its count is the total number of transactions, and its balance is the sum\n   of every transaction amount:\n\n```text\nTOTAL,,<count>,<sum>\n```\n\n5. With no accounts, print only `TOTAL,,0,0.00` after the header.\n\nNo value is embedded as a constant. Every count and sum is derived from a query.\n\n## Routes and Interfaces\n\n| Interface | Kind | Notes |\n|---|---|---|\n| `ledger report balances` | CLI command | Exit 0 on success. |\n\n## Reads and Writes\n\n| Operation | Access | Repository operation | Notes |\n|---|---|---|---|\n| List accounts | read | `accounts.list` | Ordered by code. |\n| List transactions | read | `transactions.list` | Aggregated per account and in total. |\n\n## Programmatic Acceptance\n\n=== AC report-balances-header ===\nIntent: The balances report begins with the account header line.\nRequires: executable=python3; scope=test\n\nfrom persistence.testing import db_session\nfrom cli.testing import cli_runner\n\nwith db_session() as db:\n    db.accounts.create(account=\"RBA1\", name=\"Report Balance A\")\n    code, out, err = cli_runner(\"report\", \"balances\")\n    assert code == 0\n    assert out.splitlines()[0] == \"account,name,transactions,balance\"\n=== END AC report-balances-header ===\n\n=== AC report-balances-empty ===\nIntent: With no accounts the report prints only the TOTAL row of zeros.\nRequires: executable=python3; scope=test\n\nfrom persistence.testing import db_session\nfrom cli.testing import cli_runner\n\nwith db_session() as db:\n    db.accounts.create(account=\"RBB1\", name=\"Report Balance B\")\n    db.categories.create(category=\"RBC1\", description=\"Report Category\")\n    db.transactions.create(txn_id=7001, date=\"2026-05-01\", account=\"RBB1\",\n                           category=\"RBC1\", amount=1250, memo=\"a\")\n    db.transactions.create(txn_id=7002, date=\"2026-05-02\", account=\"RBB1\",\n                           category=\"RBC1\", amount=-250, memo=\"b\")\n    code, out, err = cli_runner(\"report\", \"balances\")\n    assert code == 0\n    rows = out.splitlines()\n    assert rows[1] == \"RBB1,Report Balance B,2,10.00\"\n=== END AC report-balances-row-values ===\n\n=== AC report-balances-ordered-by-code ===\nIntent: Account rows are ordered by account code ascending.\nRequires: executable=python3; Within the command transaction, read every category through `categories.list`, ordered by\n   `category` ascending.\n2. For each category, compute by query over `transactions.list`:\n   - `transactions`: the number of rows in that category.\n   - `inflow`: the sum of positive amounts, in integer cents.\n   - `outflow`: the sum of the absolute values of negative amounts, in integer cents, printed as a\n     positive number.\n   - `net`: `inflow - outflow`.\n   A category with no transactions reports `0`, `0.00`, `0.00`, `0.00`.\n3. Print the header and one row per category:\n\n```text\ncategory,description,transactions,inflow,outflow,net\n```\n\n4. Print the TOTAL row, whose count, inflow, outflow, and net are summed over every transaction:\n\n```text\nTOTAL,,<count>,<inflow>,<outflow>,<net>\n```\n\nAmounts are formatted per FEATURE-Amount-Format.md. No value is embedded as a constant.\n\n## Routes and Interfaces\n\n| Interface | Kind | Notes |\n|---|---|---|\n| `ledger report categories` | CLI command | Exit 0 on success. |\n\n## Reads and Writes\n\n| Operation | Access | Repository operation | Notes |\n|---|---|---|---|\n| List categories | read | `categories.list` | Ordered by code; includes empty categories. |\n| List transactions | read | `transactions.list` | Aggregated per category and in total. |\n\n## Programmatic Acceptance\n\n=== AC report-categories-header ===\nIntent: The categories report begins with the category header line.\nRequires: executable=python3; Within the command transaction, read every transaction through `transactions.list`.\n2. Group rows by month, the `YYYY-MM` prefix of `date`. Only months that have transactions appear.\n3. For each month, compute by query:\n   - `transactions`: the number of rows in that month.\n   - `inflow`: the sum of positive amounts, in integer cents.\n   - `outflow`: the sum of the absolute values of negative amounts, in integer cents, printed as a\n     positive number.\n   - `net`: `inflow - outflow`.\n4. Print the header, then one row per month in ascending `YYYY-MM` order:\n\n```text\nmonth,transactions,inflow,outflow,net\n```\n\n5. Print the TOTAL row, whose count, inflow, outflow, and net are summed over every transaction:\n\n```text\nTOTAL,<count>,<inflow>,<outflow>,<net>\n```\n\n6. With no transactions, print only `TOTAL,0,0.00,0.00,0.00` after the header.\n\nAmounts are formatted per FEATURE-Amount-Format.md. No value is embedded as a constant.\n\n## Routes and Interfaces\n\n| Interface | Kind | Notes |\n|---|---|---|\n| `ledger report monthly` | CLI command | Exit 0 on success. |\n\n## Reads and Writes\n\n| Operation | Access | Repository operation | Notes |\n|---|---|---|---|\n| List transactions | read | `transactions.list` | Grouped by month and totalled in code from the query result. |\n\n## Programmatic Acceptance\n\n=== AC report-monthly-header ===\nIntent: The monthly report begins with the month header line.\nRequires: executable=python3;
